# E-commerce Customer Support Knowledge Base

## Support Case 1: Mobile Number and Email Verification
Category: Login and Account > Verification requirement for mobile number or email address during login

Customer question: How can I log in to my account to purchase an Oven Toaster Grill (OTG) as I'm unable to proceed due to mobile number or email verification?
Support answer: After confirming the customer's registered email address, I have sent a verification code to johndoe@email.com. The customer should enter the code in the field provided and click 'Verify'. Once the email address is verified, they will be able to proceed with their purchase.

## Support Case 2: Pickup and Shipping
Category: Cancellations and returns > Reasons for being asked to ship the item

Customer question: Why am I being asked to ship back the computer monitor I purchased last week?
Support answer: The monitor has been recalled by the manufacturer due to a technical issue. For safety reasons, you need to return the product, and you will receive a full refund.

Customer question: Can you guide me through the process of returning the monitor?
Support answer: A prepaid shipping label will be sent to you via email within 24 hours. Pack the monitor securely in its original packaging and drop it off at any UPS store. Upon receipt, the refund process will be initiated, and you will receive your money within 5-7 business days.

## Support Case 3: Replacement and Return Process
Category: Cancellations and returns > Inability to click the 'Cancel' button

Customer question: I am unable to click the 'Cancel' button for my Juicer/Mixer/Grinder that I recently purchased from your website.
Support answer: The 'Cancel' button might not be working due to a technical glitch. However, as you have opted for a replacement, you don't need to worry about it. Just follow the replacement process, and we will take care of the rest.

## Support Case 4: Login Issues and Error Messages
Category: Login and Account > Error message regarding exceeded attempts to enter the correct verification code

Customer question: What is the issue I am facing?
Support answer: The agent understood that the customer was facing issues while logging into their account and was getting an error message saying that they had exceeded the number of attempts to enter the correct verification code.

Customer question: What is the agent asking for?
Support answer: The agent asked for the customer's email address and the order number for the water purifier to check the customer's account.

Customer question: What did the agent do after checking the account?
Support answer: The agent suggested that the customer wait for a few minutes and try logging in again. If the customer was still unable to log in, the agent offered to reset the customer's password and send a new verification code.

Customer question: What did the agent do after the customer confirmed that they were still unable to log in?
Support answer: The agent reset the customer's password and sent a new verification code to the customer's phone number.

Customer question: What was the final solution provided by the agent?
Support answer: The agent reset the customer's password and sent a new verification code to the customer's phone number, which allowed the customer to log in successfully.

## Support Case 26: Accessing Warranty Details

Customer question: How can I access the warranty details for my new Nikon D5600 camera?
Support answer: To access the warranty details for your Nikon D5600 camera, log in to your BrownBox account, navigate to the 'My Orders' section, select your order number, and view the details. If you can't find the 'My Orders' section, log in to your account by clicking 'Sign In' on the top right corner of the website, then click on your name and select 'My Account' from the drop-down menu. If you still can't find the warranty details, check the warranty card that came with the camera for the warranty period and manufacturer contact information.

Customer question: How can I sign up for a dishwasher on the BrownBox website using my international number?

Then, click on the 'Continue' button. On the next page, enter your international number in the format '+[country code][phone number]' and click on the 'Send Verification Code' button. You have successfully signed up using your international number.

Customer question: Can you explain the coverage of my warranty?

If the problem cannot be resolved, we will replace your product or issue a refund.

Customer question: What if I accidentally damage the product?

The plan covers accidental damage, including drops, spills, and cracked screens. The cost of the plan is $XX, and you can purchase it within 30 days of your original purchase.

## Support Case 604: Login Methods
Category: Login and Account > Logging in with a mobile number on the mobile app

Customer question: How can I log in to the mobile app with my mobile number?
Support answer: To log in with your mobile number, please follow these steps: 
 Step 1: Open the BrownBox mobile app and click on the 'Login' button. 
 Step 2: Select the 'Mobile Number' option. 
 Step 3: Enter your mobile number and click on the 'Send OTP' button. 
 Step 4: You'll receive a One-Time Password (OTP) on your mobile number. Enter the OTP on the app and click on the 'Verify' button. 
 Step 5: You'll be logged in to your account, and you can proceed with your purchase.
